```python
"""
Search for non-negative step-function heights a minimizing
    2*n*max(convolve(a,a)) / sum(a)^2.

This implementation combines several complementary ideas:

1. FFT-based surrogate scoring, so long candidate sequences can be handled
   quickly.
2. A family of constructive starting points:
   - interpolated previous best,
   - smooth parametric densities,
   - random log-Fourier densities,
   - spectral phase-retrieval candidates for parametric target convolutions
     with controlled area.
3. A "clip-and-realize" projection loop: clip the current convolution toward
   a lower peak, then realize that clipped target by spectral phase retrieval
   and non-negative projection.
4. Adam-style descent in log-space for the soft maximum of the convolution,
   with backtracking line search, donor restarts, and smooth frequency-domain
   perturbations.

The best sequence found under the time budget is returned.
"""

import time
import numpy as np


def _pair_score(pair):
    return pair[0]


def _next_pow2(x):
    x = int(max(1, x))
    p = 1
    while p < x:
        p <<= 1
    return p


def _proj_nonneg_sum(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel().copy()
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(1, dtype=np.float64)
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    s = float(a.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 1e-300:
        a = np.ones(a.size, dtype=np.float64)
        return a / float(a.size)
    return a / s


def _clean_v(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    v = v - float(np.mean(v))
    return np.clip(v, -35.0, 35.0)


def _soft(v):
    v = np.asarray(v, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    v = np.where(np.isfinite(v), v, 0.0)
    m = float(np.max(v))
    if not np.isfinite(m):
        return np.full(v.shape, 1.0 / max(1, v.size), dtype=np.float64)
    x = v - m
    x = np.clip(x, -745.0, 700.0)
    e = np.exp(x)
    s = float(e.sum())
    if not np.isfinite(s) or s <= 0.0:
        return np.full(v.shape, 1.0 / max(1, v.size), dtype=np.float64)
    return e / s


def _log_plus(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    return np.log(np.maximum(a, 1e-300))


def _score_from_F(a, F, m):
    n = int(a.size)
    if n == 0:
        return np.inf
    L = 2 * n - 1
    b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
    np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
    bm = float(np.max(b)) if b.size else 0.0
    S = float(np.sum(a))
    if not np.isfinite(bm) or bm < 0.0 or S <= 1e-300 or not np.isfinite(S):
        return np.inf
    return float(2.0 * n * bm / (S * S))


def _score_m(a, m):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = _next_pow2(2 * int(a.size) - 1)
    F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
    return _score_from_F(a, F, m)


def score_fft(a):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.inf
    m = _next_pow2(2 * int(a.size) - 1)
    return _score_m(a, m)


def _interp(a, n):
    a = np.asarray(a, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
    n = int(max(1, n))
    a = np.where(np.isfinite(a), a, 0.0)
    a = np.maximum(a, 0.0)
    if a.size == 0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    if a.size == n:
        return _proj_nonneg_sum(a)
    if float(a.sum()) <= 0.0:
        return np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
    x_old = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, a.size)
    x_new = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
    out = np.interp(x_new, x_old, a)
    return _proj_nonneg_sum(out)


def _clean_sequence_list(arrs):
    out = []
    for a in arrs:
        try:
            out.append(_proj_nonneg_sum(a))
        except Exception:
            pass
    return out


def _score_normalize_list(arrs, max_keep=None, thresh=0.04):
    scored = []
    seen = []
    limit = 90
    for a in arrs:
        if len(scored) >= limit:
            break
        try:
            a = _proj_nonneg_sum(a)
            sc = score_fft(a)
        except Exception:
            continue
        if not np.isfinite(sc):
            continue
        dup = False
        for b in seen:
            try:
                d = float(np.linalg.norm(a - b))
            except Exception:
                d = 1.0
            if d < thresh:
                dup = True
                break
        if not dup:
            scored.append((sc, a))
            seen.append(a.copy())
    scored.sort(key=_pair_score)
    if max_keep is not None and len(scored) > max_keep:
        scored = scored[:max_keep]
    return scored


def _make_param_inits(n, rng, prev_a):
    n = int(max(3, n))
    c = []
    if prev_a is not None:
        c.append(_interp(prev_a, n))
    c.append(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))

    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    # Monotone exponentials are useful because the central convolution terms
    # can pair high and low values.
    for cc in (0.3, 0.7, 1.3, 2.0, 3.5, 6.0, 10.0, 16.0):
        c.append(np.exp(cc * x))
        c.append(np.exp(-cc * x))

    # Power laws.
    for p in (0.25, 0.5, 1.0, 2.0, 4.0):
        c.append((x + 1e-6) ** p)
        c.append((1.0 - x + 1e-6) ** p)

    # Some beta-like shapes.
    for p, q in ((0.5, 0.5), (0.25, 1.0), (1.0, 0.25), (0.1, 0.5), (0.5, 0.1)):
        c.append((x + 1e-6) ** p * (1.0 - x + 1e-6) ** q)

    # Low-order polynomials.
    z = 2.0 * x - 1.0
    for A in (-0.9, -0.5, -0.2, 0.2, 0.5, 0.9):
        for B in (-0.4, 0.0, 0.4):
            c.append(np.maximum(1.0 + A * z + B * z * z, 0.0))

    K = min(10, max(2, n // 64))

    # Random positive log-ish densities.
    for _ in range(8):
        v = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64)
        v += rng.normal(0.0, 0.5) * x
        for k in range(1, K + 1):
            amp = rng.uniform(0.2, 1.0) / (k ** 0.6)
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            v += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph)
        c.append(np.maximum(v, 1e-3))

    # Random exponentiated Fourier.
    for _ in range(6):
        v = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
        v += rng.normal(0.0, 0.4) * x
        for k in range(1, K + 1):
            amp = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.8) / (k ** 0.7)
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            v += amp * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph)
            v += amp * rng.uniform(0.0, 1.0) * np.sin(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi))
        v = np.clip(v, -20.0, 20.0)
        c.append(np.exp(v))

    # Smooth modulations of previous best.
    if prev_a is not None:
        base = _proj_nonneg_sum(_interp(prev_a, n))
        for _ in range(6):
            k = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, min(8, n // 16))))
            ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
            rho = rng.uniform(0.05, 0.5)
            mod = 1.0 + rho * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph)
            c.append(np.maximum(base * mod, 0.0))

    return _clean_sequence_list(c)


def _target_coefficients(n, h, power):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    L = 2 * n - 1
    c = float(n - 1)
    d = max(1.0, c)
    ay = np.abs(np.arange(L, dtype=np.float64) - c) / d
    h = float(np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0 - 1e-9))
    T = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
    mask = ay > h
    if np.any(mask):
        t = (ay[mask] - h) / (1.0 - h)
        t = np.clip(t, 0.0, 1.0)
        if power <= 1.0:
            T[mask] = 1.0 - t
        else:
            T[mask] = (1.0 - t) ** power
    T = np.maximum(T, 0.0)
    if T.size >= 2:
        T[0] = 0.0
        T[-1] = 0.0
    return T


def _target_with_area(n, area, power):
    """
    Return a target convolution shape normalized to total area 1.

    For a raw shape with maximum 1 and area A, the normalized maximum is 1/A.
    Hence a normalized target with area A would idealize score 2/A.
    """
    n = int(max(2, n))
    area = float(np.clip(area, 1.0, 2.0))
    p = float(max(1.0, power))
    # Continuous approximation for flat half-width h and taper (1-t)^p:
    # A ~ 2/(p+1) + 2*h*p/(p+1)
    h = ((area - 2.0 / (p + 1.0)) * (p + 1.0)) / (2.0 * p)
    h = float(np.clip(h, 0.0, 1.0 - 1e-8))
    T = _target_coefficients(n, h, p)
    s = float(T.sum())
    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        T = np.ones(2 * n - 1, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(T.sum())
    return T / s


def _spectral_factor(target, a0, n, m, iters=30, freq_lam=0.85, time_blend=0.70):
    """
    Spectral phase retrieval for target convolution.

    It tries to find F with F^2 ~= FFT(target), preserving non-negativity
    in the time domain by projection and blending.
    """
    n = int(max(2, n))
    L = 2 * n - 1
    m = _next_pow2(m)

    T = np.fft.fft(target, m)
    if T.size != m:
        T = np.resize(T, m)

    D = np.sqrt(T + 0j)
    if not np.all(np.isfinite(D)):
        a = _proj_nonneg_sum(a0)
        return a, score_fft(a)

    a = _proj_nonneg_sum(a0)
    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_m(a, m)

    iters = int(max(1, iters))
    for it in range(iters):
        F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(F)):
            break

        # Choose the square-root branch locally closest to the current phase.
        align = np.real(D * np.conj(F))
        sign = np.where(align >= 0.0, 1.0, -1.0)
        D_aligned = sign * D

        lam = float(freq_lam) * (1.0 - 0.25 * float(it) / float(iters))
        Fnew = (1.0 - lam) * F + lam * D_aligned
        if not np.all(np.isfinite(Fnew)):
            break

        anew = np.fft.ifft(Fnew).real[:n]
        pos = np.maximum(anew, 0.0)
        ps = float(pos.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 1e-12:
            if it > 4:
                # Restart this spectral run a little closer to current shape.
                Fnew = F
                anew = np.fft.ifft(Fnew).real[:n]
                pos = np.maximum(anew, 0.0)
                ps = float(pos.sum())
                if not np.isfinite(ps) or ps <= 1e-12:
                    break
            else:
                continue

        pos = pos / ps
        anew = (1.0 - time_blend) * a + time_blend * pos
        anew = np.maximum(anew, 1e-12)
        anew = anew / float(anew.sum())

        sc = _score_m(anew, m)
        if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_sc:
            best_sc = sc
            best = anew.copy()
        a = anew

    return best, float(best_sc)


def _add_spectral_target_candidates(cands, base_a, n, m, deadline):
    n = int(max(2, n))
    areas = (
        (1.25, 1.0),
        (1.33, 1.0),
        (1.42, 1.0),
        (1.55, 1.0),
        (1.80, 1.0),
        (1.38, 2.0),
        (1.62, 2.0),
    )
    iters = 38 if n <= 1024 else (30 if n <= 4096 else 22)
    base = _proj_nonneg_sum(base_a)

    for area, power in areas:
        if time.monotonic() >= deadline:
            break
        try:
            T = _target_with_area(n, area, power)
            a2, _ = _spectral_factor(
                T,
                base,
                n,
                m,
                iters=iters,
                freq_lam=0.85,
                time_blend=0.70
            )
            cands.append(a2)

            if n <= 2048 and time.monotonic() < deadline:
                a3, _ = _spectral_factor(
                    T,
                    base[::-1],
                    n,
                    m,
                    iters=iters,
                    freq_lam=0.65,
                    time_blend=0.55
                )
                cands.append(a3)
        except Exception:
            pass

    return cands


def _clip_target_from_b(b, bmax, goal_bmax):
    L = len(b)
    if L == 0:
        return np.ones(1)
    if bmax <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(bmax):
        return np.ones(L) / float(L)

    ratio = bmax / max(goal_bmax, 1e-300)
    if ratio > 1.5:
        frac = 0.97
    elif ratio > 1.2:
        frac = 0.985
    elif ratio > 1.05:
        frac = 0.993
    else:
        frac = 0.997

    M = bmax * frac
    M = max(M, goal_bmax)

    T = np.minimum(b, M)
    s = float(T.sum())
    for _ in range(5):
        if s > 0.0 and np.isfinite(s):
            D = float(T.max()) / s
            if np.isfinite(D) and D < bmax * 0.998:
                break
        M *= 0.95
        if M <= goal_bmax:
            M = goal_bmax
        T = np.minimum(b, M)
        s = float(T.sum())

    if s <= 0.0 or not np.isfinite(s):
        T = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64)
        s = float(T.sum())

    return T / s


def _clip_spectral(a0, time_limit, seed, goal_score=1.495):
    """
    Clip-and-realize loop.

    Repeatedly lower the peaks in the current convolution and use spectral
    phase retrieval to find a non-negative sequence whose convolution follows
    the lowered, normalized target.
    """
    a = _proj_nonneg_sum(a0)
    n = int(a.size)
    if n <= 2:
        return a, 2.0
    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**31 - 1))

    best = a.copy()
    best_sc = _score_m(a, m)
    if not np.isfinite(best_sc):
        best = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        best_sc = 2.0

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(5.0, float(time_limit))
    last_improve = start
    progress = 0.0

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break
        progress = min(1.0, (now - start) / max(1e-9, deadline - start))

        stuck_limit = min(max(10.0, 0.30 * float(time_limit)), 60.0)
        if now - last_improve > stuck_limit:
            # Last resort: try parametric area targets.
            improved = False
            base_area = 2.0 / max(1.40, best_sc * 0.98)
            candidates = (
                (base_area * 0.98, 1.0),
                (base_area, 1.0),
                (base_area * 1.05, 1.0),
                (base_area * 1.02, 2.0),
            )
            for area, power in candidates:
                try:
                    T = _target_with_area(n, area, power)
                    a2, sc2 = _spectral_factor(
                        T,
                        a,
                        n,
                        m,
                        iters=35 if n <= 4096 else 26,
                        freq_lam=0.85,
                        time_blend=0.70
                    )
                except Exception:
                    continue
                if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < best_sc:
                    best = a2
                    best_sc = sc2
                    a = a2
                    last_improve = now
                    improved = True
                    break

            if not improved:
                # Smooth restart from the best known sequence.
                aa = _proj_nonneg_sum(best)
                x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)
                k = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, min(14, n // 16))))
                ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
                rho = 0.05 + 0.40 * (1.0 - progress)
                aa = np.maximum(aa * (1.0 + rho * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph)), 0.0)
                a = _proj_nonneg_sum(aa)
                last_improve = now
            continue

        F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
        b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
        np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
        bmax = float(np.max(b))
        S = float(a.sum())
        cur_sc = float(2.0 * n * bmax / (S * S)) if S > 1e-300 else np.inf
        if not np.isfinite(cur_sc):
            break

        if cur_sc < best_sc - 1e-12:
            best_sc = cur_sc
            best = a.copy()
            last_improve = now

        goal_bmax = float(goal_score) / (2.0 * float(n))
        if bmax <= goal_bmax * 1.001:
            area = 2.0 / max(1.40, best_sc * 0.995)
            T = _target_with_area(n, area, 1.0)
        else:
            T = _clip_target_from_b(b, bmax, goal_bmax)

        iters = 24 if n <= 2048 else (20 if n <= 8192 else 15)
        try:
            a2, sc2 = _spectral_factor(
                T,
                a,
                n,
                m,
                iters=iters,
                freq_lam=0.85,
                time_blend=0.70
            )
        except Exception:
            a2 = a.copy()
            sc2 = cur_sc

        accepted = False
        if np.isfinite(sc2) and sc2 < cur_sc:
            a = a2
            accepted = True
            if sc2 < best_sc - 1e-12:
                best = a2.copy()
                best_sc = sc2
                last_improve = now

        if not accepted:
            tried = (0.5, 0.7, 0.3)
            for bl in tried:
                try:
                    ab = _proj_nonneg_sum(bl * a + (1.0 - bl) * a2)
                    scb = _score_m(ab, m)
                    if np.isfinite(scb) and scb < cur_sc:
                        a = ab
                        cur_sc = scb
                        accepted = True
                        if scb < best_sc - 1e-12:
                            best = ab.copy()
                            best_sc = scb
                            last_improve = now
                        break
                except Exception:
                    pass

            if not accepted:
                try:
                    ag = _proj_nonneg_sum(np.sqrt(np.maximum(a * a2, 0.0)))
                    scg = _score_m(ag, m)
                    if np.isfinite(scg) and scg < cur_sc:
                        a = ag
                        cur_sc = scg
                        accepted = True
                        if scg < best_sc - 1e-12:
                            best = ag.copy()
                            best_sc = scg
                            last_improve = now
                except Exception:
                    pass

        if not accepted and cur_sc > best_sc * 1.02:
            a = best.copy()

    return best, float(best_sc)


def optimize_seq(a0, time_limit, seed, donors=None, goal_score=1.49):
    """
    Log-space Adam descent on the soft max of the convolution.

    Uses a backtracking acceptance rule: each trial step must not increase the
    current score.  Donor sequences are optionally used for restarts.
    """
    a0 = _proj_nonneg_sum(a0)
    n = int(a0.size)
    if n <= 2:
        return a0, 2.0

    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)
    L = 2 * n - 1
    rng = np.random.default_rng(int(seed) % (2**31 - 1))
    x = np.linspace(0.0, 1.0, n)

    a = a0.copy()
    v = _clean_v(_log_plus(a))
    a = _soft(v)

    best_a = a.copy()
    best_score = _score_m(a, m)
    if not np.isfinite(best_score):
        best_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        a = best_a.copy()
        v = _clean_v(_log_plus(a))
        best_score = 2.0
    v_best = v.copy()

    mu = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    nu = np.zeros(n, dtype=np.float64)
    t = 0
    beta1 = 0.90
    beta2 = 0.95
    eps = 1e-8

    start = time.monotonic()
    deadline = start + max(5.0, float(time_limit))
    last_improve = start

    while time.monotonic() < deadline:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now >= deadline:
            break
        progress = min(1.0, (now - start) / max(1e-9, deadline - start))

        stuck_limit = min(max(8.0, 0.30 * float(time_limit)), 70.0)
        if now - last_improve > stuck_limit:
            if donors is not None and len(donors) > 0 and rng.random() < 0.45:
                donor = donors[int(rng.integers(0, len(donors)))]
                a = _proj_nonneg_sum(donor)
            else:
                a = _proj_nonneg_sum(best_a)
                k = int(rng.integers(1, max(2, min(14, n // 16))))
                ph = rng.uniform(0.0, 2.0 * np.pi)
                rho = 0.05 + 0.35 * (1.0 - progress)
                a = _proj_nonneg_sum(np.maximum(a * (1.0 + rho * np.cos(2.0 * np.pi * k * x + ph)), 0.0))

            v = _clean_v(_log_plus(a))
            mu *= 0.4
            nu *= 0.4
            t = 0
            last_improve = now
            continue

        a = _soft(v)
        F = np.fft.fft(a, m)
        b = np.fft.ifft(F * F).real[:L]
        np.maximum(b, 0.0, out=b)
        bmax = float(np.max(b))
        if not np.isfinite(bmax) or bmax <= 0.0:
            break

        S = float(a.sum())
        cur_score = float(2.0 * n * bmax / (S * S)) if S > 1e-300 else np.inf
        if not np.isfinite(cur_score):
            break

        if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
            best_score = cur_score
            best_a = a.copy()
            v_best = v.copy()
            last_improve = now

        # Soft weights over convolution lags, with a sharper secondary term.
        tau = bmax * (0.18 * (1.0 - progress) + 0.015)
        if not np.isfinite(tau) or tau <= 1e-300:
            tau = max(1e-300, bmax * 0.05)

        d1 = (b - bmax) / tau
        d1 = np.clip(d1, -60.0, 60.0)
        d1 = d1 - float(np.max(d1))
        w1 = np.exp(d1)
        s1 = float(w1.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s1) or s1 <= 0.0:
            w1 = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        else:
            w1 = w1 / s1

        d2 = d1 / 0.25
        d2 = np.clip(d2, -60.0, 60.0)
        d2 = d2 - float(np.max(d2))
        w2 = np.exp(d2)
        s2 = float(w2.sum())
        if not np.isfinite(s2) or s2 <= 0.0:
            w2 = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)
        else:
            w2 = w2 / s2

        bonus = 0.15 + 0.65 * (1.0 - progress)
        w = w1 + bonus * w2
        ws = float(w.sum())
        if ws > 0.0 and np.isfinite(ws):
            w = w / ws
        else:
            w = np.ones(L, dtype=np.float64) / float(L)

        W = np.fft.fft(w, m)
        c = np.fft.ifft(W * np.conj(F)).real[:n]
        np.maximum(c, 0.0, out=c)

        cm = float(np.dot(a, c))
        gv = a * (c - cm)
        grms = float(np.sqrt(np.mean(gv * gv)))
        if not np.isfinite(grms) or grms < 1e-14:
            v = _clean_v(v + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n))
            mu *= 0.5
            nu *= 0.5
            t = 0
            continue

        gv = gv / grms
        t += 1
        mu = beta1 * mu + (1.0 - beta1) * gv
        nu = beta2 * nu + (1.0 - beta2) * (gv * gv)

        b1 = beta1 ** t
        b2 = beta2 ** t
        mhat = mu / max(1e-12, 1.0 - b1)
        vhat = nu / max(1e-12, 1.0 - b2)
        step = mhat / (np.sqrt(vhat) + eps)

        # Remove the constant direction in log-space, which does not change a.
        step = step - a * float(np.dot(step, a))
        max_step = float(np.max(np.abs(step)))
        if not np.isfinite(max_step) or max_step <= 1e-15:
            v = _clean_v(v + rng.normal(0.0, 0.05, n))
            mu *= 0.5
            nu *= 0.5
            t = 0
            continue

        step = step / max_step
        lr = 0.14 + 0.08 * (1.0 - progress)

        accepted = False
        for mult in (1.0, 0.5, 0.25, 0.125):
            v_trial = v - mult * lr * step
            v_trial = _clean_v(v_trial)
            a_trial = _soft(v_trial)
            sc_trial = _score_m(a_trial, m)
            if np.isfinite(sc_trial) and sc_trial <= cur_score + 1e-12:
                v = v_trial
                a = a_trial
                cur_score = sc_trial
                accepted = True
                if cur_score < best_score - 1e-12:
                    best_score = cur_score
                    best_a = a.copy()
                    v_best = v.copy()
                    last_improve = now
                break

        if not accepted:
            mu *= 0.8
            nu *= 0.8
            if cur_score > best_score * 1.02:
                v = _clean_v(v_best + rng.normal(0.0, 0.03, n))
                a = _soft(v)

    a = _soft(v)
    sc = _score_m(a, m)
    if np.isfinite(sc) and sc < best_score:
        best_score = sc
        best_a = a.copy()

    return _proj_nonneg_sum(best_a), float(best_score)


def _process_stage(n, deadline, prev_a, rng):
    now = time.monotonic()
    n = int(max(64, n))
    if now >= deadline or prev_a is None:
        if prev_a is None:
            prev_a = np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n)
        return _proj_nonneg_sum(_interp(prev_a, n)), score_fft(prev_a)

    budget = max(5.0, float(deadline) - now)
    m = _next_pow2(2 * n - 1)

    # Parametric candidates.
    inits = _make_param_inits(n, rng, prev_a)
    scored_raw = _score_normalize_list(inits, max_keep=1)
    if not scored_raw:
        base = _proj_nonneg_sum(np.ones(n, dtype=np.float64) / float(n))
        return base, score_fft(base)

    cands = [scored_raw[0][1]]

    # Spectral target candidates.
    spec_budget = min(60.0, 0.18 * budget)
    spec_deadline = min(deadline, time.monotonic() + max(0.0, spec_budget))
    cands = _add_spectral_target_candidates(cands, cands[0], n, m, spec_deadline)

    cands = _score_normalize_list(cands, max_keep=5)
    if not cands:
        base = _proj_nonneg_sum(_interp(prev_a, n))
        return base, score_fft(base)

    stage_best = cands[0][1]
    stage_score = cands[0][0]

    # Clip-and-realize on the strongest candidate.
    if budget > 45.0:
        clip_budget = max(12.0, min(0.32 * budget, 0.32 * budget))
        try:
            a_clip, sc_clip = _clip_spectral(
                stage_best,
                clip_budget,
                int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1)),
                goal_score=1.495
            )
        except Exception:
            a_clip, sc_clip = stage_best, stage_score
        if np.isfinite(sc_clip) and sc_clip < stage_score:
            stage_best = a_clip
            stage_score = sc_clip

    # Direct optimization over several candidates.
    opt_deadline = max(time.monotonic(), deadline - 5.0)
    rem_total = max(0.0, opt_deadline - time.monotonic())

    if rem_total > 18.0:
        opt_cands = [stage_best]
        for sc, a in cands:
            if len(opt_cands) >= 4:
                break
            try:
                d = float(np.linalg.norm(a - stage_best))
            except Exception:
                d = 1.0
            if d > 0.035:
                opt_cands.append(a)

        weights = (0.45, 0.20, 0.17, 0.18)
        for i, cand in enumerate(opt_cands):
            if time.monotonic() >= opt_deadline - 10.0:
                break
            alloc = rem_total * (weights[i] if i < len(weights) else 0.15)
            if alloc < 8.0:
                continue
            seed = int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1))
            try:
                donor_list = opt_cands if len(opt_cands) > 1 else None
                a_opt, sc_opt = optimize_seq(
                    cand,
                    alloc,
                    seed,
                    donors=donor_list
                )
            except Exception:
                a_opt, sc_opt = cand, stage_score
            if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < stage_score:
                stage_best = a_opt
                stage_score = sc_opt

        # Final polish within this stage.
        rem = max(0.0, opt_deadline - time.monotonic())
        if rem > 12.0:
            seed = int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1))
            try:
                a_opt, sc_opt = optimize_seq(stage_best, rem, seed)
            except Exception:
                a_opt, sc_opt = stage_best, stage_score
            if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < stage_score:
                stage_best = a_opt
                stage_score = sc_opt

    # Make sure we return something finite for this stage.
    if stage_best is None or stage_score != stage_score or not np.isfinite(stage_score):
        stage_best = _proj_nonneg_sum(_interp(prev_a, n))
        stage_score = score_fft(stage_best)

    return stage_best, float(stage_score)


def _load_previous():
    try:
        cand = height_sequence_1  # noqa: F821
        arr = np.asarray(cand, dtype=np.float64).ravel()
        if arr.size > 0 and np.all(np.isfinite(arr)):
            arr = np.maximum(arr, 0.0)
            s = float(arr.sum())
            if s > 0.0:
                return arr / s
    except Exception:
        pass
    return None


def propose_candidate(seed=42, budget_s=2400, **kwargs):
    """
    Entry point.  Searches for the best non-negative sequence under budget_s.
    """
    seed = int(seed)
    budget_s = float(max(60.0, budget_s))
    start = time.monotonic()
    global_deadline = start + max(60.0, budget_s - 30.0)
    rng = np.random.default_rng(seed)

    prev = _load_previous()
    if prev is None:
        best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
        best_score = 2.0
    else:
        best_a = _proj_nonneg_sum(prev)
        best_score = score_fft(best_a)
        if not np.isfinite(best_score):
            best_a = np.ones(1024, dtype=np.float64) / 1024.0
            best_score = 2.0

    print("Initial best score: %.8f (len=%d)" % (best_score, len(best_a)))

    base = max(60.0, budget_s - 30.0)
    # Fractions of total budget.  The small stages mainly discover good
    # envelopes and orientations; the larger stages polish them.
    stage_defs = (
        (512, 0.02),
        (1024, 0.05),
        (2048, 0.12),
        (4096, 0.32),
        (8192, 0.38),
        (16384, 0.11),
    )

    for n, frac in stage_defs:
        now = time.monotonic()
        if now + 30.0 >= global_deadline:
            break
        alloc = base * float(frac)
        stage_deadline = min(global_deadline - 5.0, now + alloc)
        if stage_deadline <= now:
            continue

        prev_stage = _interp(best_a, int(n))
        print("Stage n=%d budget=%.1fs current_best=%.8f" % (n, alloc, best_score))
        try:
            a_new, sc_new = _process_stage(int(n), stage_deadline, prev_stage, rng)
        except Exception as e:
            print("Stage n=%d failed: %s" % (n, e))
            a_new, sc_new = prev_stage, score_fft(prev_stage)

        if a_new is not None and np.isfinite(sc_new) and sc_new < best_score:
            best_a = _proj_nonneg_sum(a_new)
            best_score = float(sc_new)
        print("After stage n=%d best score = %.8f (len=%d)" % (n, best_score, len(best_a)))

    # Final polish on the best sequence found so far.
    rem = max(0.0, global_deadline - time.monotonic())
    if best_a is not None and rem > 25.0:
        seed2 = int(rng.integers(0, 2**31 - 1))
        try:
            a_opt, sc_opt = optimize_seq(best_a, rem, seed2)
        except Exception as e:
            print("Final polish failed: %s" % e)
            a_opt, sc_opt = best_a, best_score
        if np.isfinite(sc_opt) and sc_opt < best_score:
            best_a = a_opt
            best_score = float(sc_opt)

    best_a = _proj_nonneg_sum(best_a)

    # Optional exact validation for reasonably sized final sequences.
    try:
        if len(best_a) <= 8192:
            exact = evaluate_sequence([float(x) for x in best_a])  # noqa: F821
            print("Final FFT score=%.8f exact_eval_score=%.8f len=%d" %
                  (best_score, exact, len(best_a)))
            if not np.isfinite(exact):
                fallback = _proj_nonneg_sum(np.ones(2048, dtype=np.float64) / 2048.0)
                return [float(x) for x in fallback]
        else:
            print("Final FFT score=%.8f len=%d (exact eval omitted to avoid quadratic cost)" %
                  (best_score, len(best_a)))
    except Exception as e:
        print("Exact validation skipped: %s" % e)

    return [float(x) for x in best_a]
```
